if (item.stock !== null && item.stock > 0 && item.status === 'sold-out') item.status = 'available'; } function loadAnnouncement(){ try{ const saved = localStorage.getItem('solaAnnouncement'); if (saved) return { ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_ANNOUNCEMENT)), ...JSON.parse(saved) }; } } function getItemKey(group, item){ return (group && group.title ? group.title : '') + '::' + (item && item.name ? item.name : ''); } function loadPopularity(){ try{ const saved = localStorage.getItem('solaPopularity'); } function escapeHtml(str){ return String(str == null ? '' : str) .replace(/&/g,'&').replace(//g,'>'); } function escapeAttr(str){ return escapeHtml(str).replace(/"/g,'"'); } let menuData = normalizeMenuData(loadMenu()); menuData.forEach(group => group.items.forEach(item => syncInventoryStatus(item))); let announcementData = loadAnnouncement(); let galleryData = loadGallery(); let contactData = loadContact(); let siteSettings = loadSiteSettings(); let popularityData = loadPopularity(); let isAdmin = sessionStorage.getItem('solaAdmin') === '1'; function getStatusMeta(status){ const map = { available: { label:'Available', short:'Available', cls:'available' }, 'sold-out': { label:'Sold Out', short:'Sold Out', cls:'sold-out' }, 'not-available': { label:'Not Available', short:'Not Available', cls:'not-available' }, 'coming-soon': { label:'Coming Soon', short:'Coming Soon', cls:'coming-soon' } }; return map[status] || map.available; } function getAllMenuItems(){ const result = []; menuData.forEach((group, gi) => group.items.forEach((item, ii) => { result.push({ item, group, gi, ii }); })); return result; } function getPriceNumber(price){ const match = String(price || '').replace(/,/g,'').match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/); return match ? if (smartFilters.student && !item.studentPick) return false; return true; } function updateSmartResultsNote(total){ const el = document.getElementById('smartResultsNote'); if (!el) return; const parts = []; if (e.key === 'Escape') closeItemPhotoModal(); }); // Reads an image file from disk, downsizes it so it stays a reasonable // size to store and publish, and returns a compressed JPEG data URL. function resizeImageFile(file, maxDim, quality){ return new Promise((resolve, reject) => { const reader = new FileReader(); reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Could not read file')); reader.onload = (e) => { const img = new Image(); img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Could not decode image')); img.onload = () => { let w = img.naturalWidth, h = img.naturalHeight; if (w > maxDim || h > maxDim){ if (w >= h){ h = Math.round(h * maxDim / w); w = maxDim; } else { w = Math.round(w * maxDim / h); h = maxDim; } } const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = w; canvas.height = h; canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h); resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality)); }; img.src = e.target.result; }; reader.readAsDataURL(file); }); } function applyContactLinks(){ const callBtn = document.getElementById('callBtn'); }); // ---------- Weather (Open-Meteo, no API key needed) ---------- const WEATHER_CODES = { 0:['☀️','Clear sky'], 1:['🌤️','Mostly clear'], 2:['⛅','Partly cloudy'], 3:['☁️','Overcast'], 45:['🌫️','Foggy'], 48:['🌫️','Foggy'], 51:['🌦️','Light drizzle'], 53:['🌦️','Drizzle'], 55:['🌧️','Heavy drizzle'], 61:['🌧️','Light rain'], 63:['🌧️','Rain'], 65:['🌧️','Heavy rain'], 71:['🌨️','Light snow'], 73:['🌨️','Snow'], 75:['❄️','Heavy snow'], 80:['🌦️','Rain showers'], 81:['🌧️','Rain showers'], 82:['⛈️','Violent showers'], 95:['⛈️','Thunderstorm'], 96:['⛈️','Thunderstorm w/ hail'], 99:['⛈️','Thunderstorm w/ hail'] }; function parseLatLon(text){ const m = String(text||'').trim().match(/^(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*,\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)$/); if (!m) return null; return { lat: Number(m[1]), lon: Number(m[2]) }; } async function fetchWeather(){ const chip = document.getElementById('weatherChip'); if (!chip) return; const coords = parseLatLon(contactData.mapsQuery) || { lat: 11.4878, lon: 122.9956 }; // Pilar, Capiz fallback try{ const cacheKey = 'solaWeatherCache'; const cached = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(cacheKey) || 'null'); let data; if (cached && cached.lat === coords.lat && cached.lon === coords.lon && (Date.now() - cached.time) < 30*60*1000){ data = cached.data; } else { const res = await fetch('https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude='+coords.lat+'&longitude='+coords.lon+'¤t_weather=true'); if (!res.ok) throw new Error('weather fetch failed'); data = await res.json(); sessionStorage.setItem(cacheKey, JSON.stringify({ lat: coords.lat, lon: coords.lon, time: Date.now(), data })); } const cw = data.current_weather; const meta = WEATHER_CODES[cw.weathercode] || ['🌡️','Weather']; document.getElementById('weatherIcon').textContent = meta[0]; document.getElementById('weatherText').textContent = Math.round(cw.temperature) + '°C · ' + meta[1]; chip.hidden = false; }catch(e){ chip.hidden = true; } } // ---------- In-page QR code (links to this same page) ---------- function setSiteQrCode(){ const img = document.getElementById('siteQrCode'); Try again in ' + wait + 's.'); return; } const pw = prompt('Enter admin password:'); if (pw === null) return; const enteredHash = await hashText(pw); if (enteredHash === ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH){ setAdminAttempts({ count:0, until:0 }); setAdminMode(true); } else { const count = attempts.count + 1; const until = count >= 5 ? Date.now() + ADMIN_LOCKOUT_MS : 0; setAdminAttempts({ count, until }); alert(until ? 'Incorrect password. Too many attempts — locked for 30s.' : 'Incorrect password.'); } }); document.getElementById('addGroupBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { menuData.push({ title: 'New Group', desc: '', items: [{ name: 'New item', desc: '', price: '₱0' }] }); saveMenuLocal(); renderMenu(); }); document.getElementById('resetMenuBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { if (confirm('Reset the menu, gallery, and contact info to the version currently published in this file? This clears your local edits on this device.')){ localStorage.removeItem('solaMenuData'); localStorage.removeItem('solaGalleryData'); localStorage.removeItem('solaContactData'); localStorage.removeItem('solaAnnouncement'); menuData = normalizeMenuData(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_MENU))); galleryData = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_GALLERY)); contactData = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_CONTACT)); announcementData = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_ANNOUNCEMENT)); renderMenu(); renderGallery(); applyContactLinks(); renderContactAdminFields(); } }); // ===================================================================== // PUBLISH LIVE — commits the updated menu straight to your GitHub repo // via the GitHub API, so GitHub Pages rebuilds the live site for every // visitor without you needing to download/upload anything by hand. // // Change these three to match your repo (from a URL like // github.com/OWNER/REPO, with this file at FILE_PATH): // ===================================================================== const GITHUB_OWNER = "bagobo101"; const GITHUB_REPO = "Sola-Cafe"; const GITHUB_FILE_PATH = "index.html"; function utf8ToBase64(str){ return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(str))); } function base64ToUtf8(str){ return decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(str))); } function getGithubToken(promptIfMissing){ let token = sessionStorage.getItem('solaGhToken'); if (!token && promptIfMissing){ token = prompt( 'Paste your GitHub personal access token.\n\n' + 'It needs "Contents: Read and write" access on the ' + GITHUB_OWNER + '/' + GITHUB_REPO + ' repo.\n' + 'Create one at github.com/settings/personal-access-tokens/new\n\n' + 'This token is kept only in this browser tab (never saved to the file or sent anywhere but GitHub).' ); if (token) sessionStorage.setItem('solaGhToken', token.trim()); } return token ? token.trim() : token; } async function publishToGithub(){ const token = getGithubToken(true); if (!token) return; const btn = document.getElementById('publishBtn'); const originalLabel = btn.textContent; btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Publishing…'; try{ const apiBase = 'https://api.github.com/repos/' + GITHUB_OWNER + '/' + GITHUB_REPO; const headers = { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token, 'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json' }; const repoRes = await fetch(apiBase, { headers }); if (!repoRes.ok) throw new Error('Could not read repo info (status ' + repoRes.status + '). Check the owner/repo name at the top of this script and that your token has access.'); const repoInfo = await repoRes.json(); const branch = repoInfo.default_branch || 'main'; const fileRes = await fetch(apiBase + '/contents/' + GITHUB_FILE_PATH + '?ref=' + branch, { headers }); if (!fileRes.ok) throw new Error('Could not read ' + GITHUB_FILE_PATH + ' from the repo (status ' + fileRes.status + ').'); const fileInfo = await fileRes.json(); const currentHtml = base64ToUtf8(fileInfo.content); const { html: updatedHtml, missing } = injectAllData(currentHtml); if (missing.length){ throw new Error( 'Could not find these markers in the live ' + GITHUB_FILE_PATH + ': ' + missing.join('; ') + '. The live file on GitHub is missing (or has altered) these comment markers, so nothing was published. ' + 'Open index.html in your ' + GITHUB_OWNER + '/' + GITHUB_REPO + ' repo and check for them.' ); } const putRes = await fetch(apiBase + '/contents/' + GITHUB_FILE_PATH, { method: 'PUT', headers: Object.assign({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, headers), body: JSON.stringify({ message: 'Update menu, availability, announcement, gallery, and contact info via admin panel', content: utf8ToBase64(updatedHtml), sha: fileInfo.sha, branch: branch }) }); if (!putRes.ok){ const errBody = await putRes.json().catch(() => ({})); throw new Error('GitHub rejected the update (status ' + putRes.status + '): ' + (errBody.message || 'unknown error')); } alert('Published! GitHub Pages usually takes about a minute to rebuild — refresh the live site shortly to see your changes.'); } catch(err){ alert('Could not publish: ' + err.message); } finally { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = originalLabel; } } function injectAllData(html){ let out = html; const missing = []; const menuRe = /\/\*MENU_DATA_START\*\/[\s\S]*?\/\*MENU_DATA_END\*\//; if (menuRe.test(out)) { out = out.replace(menuRe, '/*MENU_DATA_START*/' + JSON.stringify(normalizeMenuData(menuData), null, 2) + '/*MENU_DATA_END*/'); } else { missing.push('MENU_DATA_START / MENU_DATA_END'); } const galleryRe = /\/\*GALLERY_DATA_START\*\/[\s\S]*?\/\*GALLERY_DATA_END\*\//; if (galleryRe.test(out)) { out = out.replace(galleryRe, '/*GALLERY_DATA_START*/' + JSON.stringify(galleryData, null, 2) + '/*GALLERY_DATA_END*/'); } else { missing.push('GALLERY_DATA_START / GALLERY_DATA_END'); } const announcementRe = /\/\*ANNOUNCEMENT_DATA_START\*\/[\s\S]*?\/\*ANNOUNCEMENT_DATA_END\*\//; if (announcementRe.test(out)) { out = out.replace(announcementRe, '/*ANNOUNCEMENT_DATA_START*/' + JSON.stringify(announcementData, null, 2) + '/*ANNOUNCEMENT_DATA_END*/'); } else { missing.push('ANNOUNCEMENT_DATA_START / ANNOUNCEMENT_DATA_END'); } const contactRe = /\/\*CONTACT_DATA_START\*\/[\s\S]*?\/\*CONTACT_DATA_END\*\//; if (contactRe.test(out)) { out = out.replace(contactRe, '/*CONTACT_DATA_START*/' + JSON.stringify(contactData, null, 2) + '/*CONTACT_DATA_END*/'); } else { missing.push('CONTACT_DATA_START / CONTACT_DATA_END'); } const siteSettingsRe = /\/\*SITE_SETTINGS_START\*\/[\s\S]*?\/\*SITE_SETTINGS_END\*\//; if (siteSettingsRe.test(out)) { out = out.replace(siteSettingsRe, '/*SITE_SETTINGS_START*/' + JSON.stringify(siteSettings, null, 2) + '/*SITE_SETTINGS_END*/'); } else { missing.push('SITE_SETTINGS_START / SITE_SETTINGS_END'); } return { html: out, missing }; } document.getElementById('publishBtn').addEventListener('click', publishToGithub); document.getElementById('forgetTokenBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { sessionStorage.removeItem('solaGhToken'); alert('Saved token cleared. You\'ll be asked to paste it again next time you publish.'); }); document.getElementById('downloadBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { const raw = document.documentElement.outerHTML; const { html, missing } = injectAllData(raw); if (missing.length){ alert('Could not find these markers in this file: ' + missing.join('; ') + '. Download cancelled so nothing gets corrupted.'); return; } const blob = new Blob(['\n' + html], { type: 'text/html' }); const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob); const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = 'index.html'; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); alert('Downloaded index.html. Upload this file to your GitHub repo (replacing the current one) to publish these menu, gallery, and contact changes for every visitor.'); }); // ---------- Light/Dark theme toggle (personal, saved on this device) ---------- function applyTheme(theme){ document.body.classList.toggle('theme-light', theme === 'light'); const btn = document.getElementById('themeToggle'); btn.textContent = theme === 'light' ? '☀️' : '🌙'; btn.setAttribute('aria-label', theme === 'light' ? 'Switch to dark mode' : 'Switch to light mode'); } let currentTheme = 'dark'; try{ currentTheme = localStorage.getItem('solaTheme') || 'dark'; }catch(e){} applyTheme(currentTheme); document.getElementById('themeToggle').addEventListener('click', () => { currentTheme = currentTheme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light'; applyTheme(currentTheme); try{ localStorage.setItem('solaTheme', currentTheme); }catch(e){} }); // ---------- Offline fallback (service worker) ---------- if ('serviceWorker' in navigator){ window.addEventListener('load', () => { navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js').catch(() => { /* sw.js not uploaded yet — safe to ignore */ }); }); } if (isAdmin) document.body.classList.add('admin-mode'); applySkin(); renderMenu(); renderGallery(); applyContactLinks(); renderContactAdminFields(); renderSiteSettingsAdmin(); renderPaymentDisplay(); setSiteQrCode(); renderAnnouncementAdmin(); renderAnnouncement(); renderDashboard();
